       showDialogFragment(dialog);\n    }\n\n    /** Selects the given position in the deck list */\n    public void selectDropDownItem(int position) {\n        mActionBarSpinner.setSelection(position);\n        deckDropDownItemChanged(position);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Performs changes relating to the Deck DropDown Item changing\n     * Exists as mActionBarSpinner.setSelection() caused a loop in roboelectirc (calling onItemSelected())\n     */\n    private void deckDropDownItemChanged(int position) {\n        if (position == 0) {\n            mRestrictOnDeck = "";\n            saveLastDeckId(ALL_DECKS_ID);\n        } else {\n            JSONObject deck = mDropDownDecks.get(position - 1);\n            mRestrictOnDeck = "deck:\\"" + deck.getString("name") + "\\" ";\n            saveLastDeckId(deck.getLong("id"));\n        }\n        searchCards();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onSaveInstanceState(Bundle savedInstanceState) {\n        // Save current search terms\n        savedInstanceState.putString("mSearchTerms", mSearchTerms);\n        super.onSaveInstanceState(savedInstanceState);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onRestoreInstanceState(Bundle savedInstanceState) {\n        super.onRestoreInstanceState(savedInstanceState);\n        mSearchTerms = savedInstanceState.getString("mSearchTerms");\n        searchCards();\n    }\n\n    private void searchCards() {\n        // cancel the previous search & render tasks if still running\n        CollectionTask.cancelTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_SEARCH_CARDS);\n        CollectionTask.cancelTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_RENDER_BROWSER_QA);\n        String searchText;\n        if (mSearchTerms == null) {\n            mSearchTerms = "";\n        }\n        if (!"".equals(mSearchTerms) && (mSearchView != null)) {\n            mSearchView.setQuery(mSearchTerms, false);\n            mSearchItem.expandActionView();\n        }\n        if (mSearchTerms.contains("deck:")) {\n            searchText = mSearchTerms;\n        } else {\n            searchText = mRestrictOnDeck + mSearchTerms;\n        }\n        if (colIsOpen() && mCardsAdapter!= null) {\n            // clear the existing card list.\n            // The checked positions refer to the list which is about to be discarded, so they must go as well,\n            // otherwise they point at cards which no longer exist (or at nothing at all): #5843\n            mCards = new ArrayList<Map<String, String>>();\n            mCheckedCardPositions.clear();\n            mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n            //  estimate maximum number of cards that could be visible (assuming worst-case minimum row height of 20dp)\n            int numCardsToRender = (int) Math.ceil(mCardsListView.getHeight()/\n                    TypedValue.applyDimension(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_DIP, 20, getResources().getDisplayMetrics())) + 5;\n            // Perform database query to get all card ids\n            CollectionTask.launchCollectionTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_SEARCH_CARDS, mSearchCardsHandler, new CollectionTask.TaskData(\n                    new Object[] {searchText, ((mOrder != CARD_ORDER_NONE)),  numCardsToRender}));\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    private void updateList() {\n        mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n        mDropDownAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n        onSelectionChanged();\n        updatePreviewMenuItem();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return text to be used in the subtitle of the drop-down deck selector\n     */\n    public String getSubtitleText() {\n        int count = getCardCount();\n        return getResources().getQuantityString(R.plurals.card_browser_subtitle, count, count);\n    }\n\n\n    private Map<Long, Integer> getPositionMap(List<Map<String, String>> list) {\n        Map<Long, Integer> positions = new HashMap<>();\n        for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {\n            positions.put(Long.valueOf(list.get(i).get(ID)), i);\n        }\n        return positions;\n    }\n\n    // Iterates the drop down decks, and selects the one matching the given id\n    private boolean selectDeckById(@NonNull Long deckId) {\n        for (int dropDownDeckIdx = 0; dropDownDeckIdx < mDropDownDecks.size(); dropDownDeckIdx++) {\n            if (mDropDownDecks.get(dropDownDeckIdx).getLong("id") == deckId) {\n                selectDropDownItem(dropDownDeckIdx + 1);\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // convenience method for updateCardsInList(...)\n    private void updateCardInList(Card card, String updatedCardTags){\n        List<Card> cards = new ArrayList<>();\n        cards.add(card);\n        if (updatedCardTags != null) {\n            Map<Long, String> updatedCardTagsMult = new HashMap<>();\n            updatedCardTagsMult.put(card.getNid(), updatedCardTags);\n            updateCardsInList(cards, updatedCardTagsMult);\n        } else {\n            updateCardsInList(cards, null);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Returns the decks which are valid targets for "Change Deck" */\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    List<JSONObject> getValidDecksForChangeDeck() {\n        List<JSONObject> nonDynamicDecks = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (JSONObject d : mDropDownDecks) {\n            if (Decks.isDynamic(d)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            nonDynamicDecks.add(d);\n        }\n        return nonDynamicDecks;\n    }\n\n\n    private void filterByTag(List<String> selectedTags, int option) {\n        //TODO: Duplication between here and CustomStudyDialog:customStudyFromTags\n        mSearchView.setQuery("", false);\n        String tags = selectedTags.toString();\n        mSearchView.setQueryHint(getResources().getString(R.string.card_browser_tags_shown,\n                tags.substring(1, tags.length() - 1)));\n        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();\n        switch (option) {\n            case 1:\n                sb.append("is:new ");\n                break;\n            case 2:\n                sb.append("is:due ");\n                break;\n            default:\n                // Logging here might be appropriate : )\n                break;\n        }\n        int i = 0;\n        for (String tag : selectedTags) {\n            if (i != 0) {\n                sb.append("or ");\n            } else {\n                sb.append("("); // Only if we really have selected tags\n            }\n            sb.append("tag:").append(tag).append(" ");\n            i++;\n        }\n        if (i > 0) {\n            sb.append(")"); // Only if we added anything to the tag list\n        }\n        mSearchTerms = sb.toString();\n        searchCards();\n    }\n\n\n    private abstract class ListenerWithProgressBar extends CollectionTask.TaskListener {\n        @Override\n        public void onPreExecute() {\n            showProgressBar();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private abstract class ListenerWithProgressBarCloseOnFalse extends ListenerWithProgressBar {\n        private String timber = null;\n\n        public ListenerWithProgressBarCloseOnFalse(String timber) {\n            this.timber = timber;\n        }\n\n        public ListenerWithProgressBarCloseOnFalse() {\n\t\t}\n\n        public void onPostExecute(CollectionTask.TaskData result) {\n            if (timber != null) {\n                Timber.d(timber);\n            }\n            if (result.getBoolean()) {\n                actualPostExecute(result);\n            } else {\n                closeCardBrowser(DeckPicker.RESULT_DB_ERROR);\n            }\n        }\n\n        protected abstract void actualPostExecute(CollectionTask.TaskData result);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @param cards Cards that were changed\n     * @param updatedCardTags Mapping note id -> updated tags\n     */\n    private void updateCardsInList(List<Card> cards, Map<Long, String> updatedCardTags) {\n        Map<Long, Integer> idToPos = getPositionMap(getCards());\n        for (Card c : cards) {\n            Note note = c.note();\n            // get position in the mCards search results HashMap\n            int pos = idToPos.containsKey(c.getId()) ? idToPos.get(c.getId()) : -1;\n            if (pos < 0 || pos >= getCardCount()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            Map<String, String> card = getCards().get(pos);\n            // update tags\n            card.put(MARKED, (c.note().hasTag("marked")) ? "marked" : null);\n            if (updatedCardTags != null) {\n                card.put(TAGS, updatedCardTags.get(c.getNid()));\n            }\n            // update sfld\n            String sfld = note.getSFld();\n            card.put(SFLD, sfld);\n            // update Q & A etc\n            updateSearchItemQA(getBaseContext(), card, c, getCol());\n            // update deck\n            String deckName;\n            deckName = getCol().getDecks().get(c.getDid()).getString("name");\n            card.put(DECK, deckName);\n            // update flags (marked / suspended / etc) which determine color\n            card.put(SUSPENDED, c.getQueue() == Consts.QUEUE_TYPE_SUSPENDED ? "True": "False");\n            card.put(FLAGS, (new Integer(c.getUserFlag())).toString());\n        }\n\n        updateList();\n    }\n\n    private CollectionTask.TaskListener mUpdateCardHandler = new ListenerWithProgressBarCloseOnFalse("Card Browser - mUpdateCardHandler.onPostExecute()"){\n        @Override\n        public void onProgressUpdate(CollectionTask.TaskData... values) {\n            updateCardInList(values[0].getCard(), values[0].getString());\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        protected void actualPostExecute(CollectionTask.TaskData result) {\n            hideProgressBar();\n        }\n    };\n\n    private CollectionTask.TaskListener mChangeDeckHandler = new ListenerWithProgressBarCloseOnFalse("Card Browser - mChangeDeckHandler.onPostExecute()") {\n        @Override\n        protected void actualPostExecute(CollectionTask.TaskData result) {\n            hideProgressBar();\n\n            searchCards();\n            endMultiSelectMode();\n            mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n            invalidateOptionsMenu();    // maybe the availability of undo changed\n\n            if (!result.getBoolean()) {\n                Timber.i("mChangeDeckHandler failed, not offering undo");\n                displayCouldNotChangeDeck();\n                return;\n            }\n            // snackbar to offer undo\n            String deckName = getCol().getDecks().name(mNewDid);\n            mUndoSnackbar = UIUtils.showSnackbar(CardBrowser.this, String.format(getString(R.string.changed_deck_message), deckName), SNACKBAR_DURATION, R.string.undo, new View.OnClickListener() {\n                @Override\n                public void onClick(View v) {\n                    CollectionTask.launchCollectionTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_UNDO, mUndoHandler);\n                }\n            }, mCardsListView, null);\n        }\n    };\n\n    public static void updateSearchItemQA(Context context, Map<String, String> item, Card c, Collection col) {\n        // render question and answer\n        Map<String, String> qa = c._getQA(true, true);\n        // Render full question / answer if the bafmt (i.e. "browser appearance") setting forced blank result\n        if ("".equals(qa.get("q")) || "".equals(qa.get("a"))) {\n            HashMap<String, String> qaFull = c._getQA(true, false);\n            if ("".equals(qa.get("q"))) {\n                qa.put("q", qaFull.get("q"));\n            }\n            if ("".equals(qa.get("a"))) {\n                qa.put("a", qaFull.get("a"));\n            }\n        }\n        // update the original hash map to include rendered question & answer\n        String q = qa.get("q");\n        String a = qa.get("a");\n        // remove the question from the start of the answer if it exists\n        if (a.startsWith(q)) {\n            a = a.replaceFirst(Pattern.quote(q), "");\n        }\n        // put all of the fields in except for those that have already been pulled out straight from the\n        // database\n        item.put(ANSWER, formatQA(a, context));\n        item.put(CARD, c.template().optString("name"));\n        item.put(DUE, c.getDueString());\n        if (c.getType() == Consts.CARD_TYPE_NEW) {\n            item.put(EASE, context.getString(R.string.card_browser_ease_new_card));\n        } else {\n            item.put(EASE, (c.getFactor()/10)+"%");\n        }\n\n        Note note = c.note();\n        item.put(CHANGED, LanguageUtil.getShortDateFormatFromS(c.getMod()));\n        item.put(CREATED, LanguageUtil.getShortDateFormatFromMs(note.getId()));\n        item.put(EDITED, LanguageUtil.getShortDateFormatFromS(note.getMod()));\n        // interval\n        switch (c.getType()) {\n            case Consts.CARD_TYPE_NEW:\n                item.put(INTERVAL, context.getString(R.string.card_browser_interval_new_card));\n                break;\n            case Consts.CARD_TYPE_LRN :\n                item.put(INTERVAL, context.getString(R.string.card_browser_interval_learning_card));\n                break;\n            default:\n                item.put(INTERVAL, Utils.roundedTimeSpanUnformatted(context, c.getIvl()*86400));\n                break;\n        }\n        item.put(LAPSES, Integer.toString(c.getLapses()));\n        item.put(NOTE, c.model().optString("name"));\n        item.put(QUESTION, formatQA(q, context));\n        item.put(REVIEWS, Integer.toString(c.getReps()));\n        String tags = note.stringTags();\n        item.put(TAGS, tags);\n        item.put(MARKED, (sMarkedPattern.matcher(item.get(TAGS)).matches())?"marked": null);\n        item.put(FLAGS, (Integer.toString(c.getUserFlag())));\n        item.put(SUSPENDED, c.getQueue() == Consts.QUEUE_TYPE_SUSPENDED ? "True": "False");\n        item.put(DECK, col.getDecks().name(c.getDid()));\n        item.put(SFLD, note.getSFld());\n    }\n\n    @CheckResult\n    private static String formatQA(String text, Context context) {\n        boolean showFilenames = AnkiDroidApp.getSharedPrefs(context).getBoolean("card_browser_show_media_filenames", false);\n        return formatQAInternal(text, showFilenames);\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * @param txt The text to strip HTML, comments, tags and media from\n     * @param showFileNames Whether [sound:foo.mp3] should be rendered as " foo.mp3 " or  " "\n     * @return The formatted string\n     */\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    @CheckResult\n    static String formatQAInternal(String txt, boolean showFileNames) {\n        /* Strips all formatting from the string txt for use in displaying question/answer in browser */\n        String s = txt;\n        s = s.replaceAll("<!--.*?-->", "");\n        s = s.replace("<br>", " ");\n        s = s.replace("<br />", " ");\n        s = s.replace("<div>", " ");\n        s = s.replace("\\n", " ");\n        s = showFileNames ? Utils.stripSoundMedia(s) : Utils.stripSoundMedia(s, " ");\n        s = s.replaceAll("\\\\[\\\\[type:[^]]+\\\\]\\\\]", "");\n        s = showFileNames ? Utils.stripHTMLMedia(s) : Utils.stripHTMLMedia(s, " ");\n        s = s.trim();\n        return s;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes cards from view. Doesn\'t delete them in model (database).\n     */\n    private void removeNotesView(Card[] cards, boolean reorderCards) {\n        List<Long> cardIds = new ArrayList<>(cards.length);\n        for (Card c : cards) {\n            cardIds.add(c.getId());\n        }\n        removeNotesView(cardIds, reorderCards);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Removes cards from view. Doesn\'t delete them in model (database).\n     * @param reorderCards Whether to rearrange the positions of checked items (DEFECT: Currently deselects all)\n     */\n    private void removeNotesView(java.util.Collection<Long> cardsIds, boolean reorderCards) {\n        long reviewerCardId = getReviewerCardId();\n        List<Map<String, String>> oldMCards = getCards();\n        Map<Long, Integer> idToPos = getPositionMap(oldMCards);\n        Set<Long> idToRemove = new HashSet<Long>();\n        for (Long cardId : cardsIds) {\n            if (cardId == reviewerCardId) {\n                mReloadRequired = true;\n            }\n            if (idToPos.containsKey(cardId)) {\n                idToRemove.add(cardId);\n            }\n        }\n\n        List<Map<String, String>> newMCards = new ArrayList<Map<String, String>>();\n        for (Map<String, String> cardProperties: oldMCards) {\n            if (! idToRemove.contains(Long.parseLong(cardProperties.get(ID)))) {\n                newMCards.add(cardProperties);\n            }\n        }\n        mCards = newMCards;\n\n        if (reorderCards) {\n            //Suboptimal from a UX perspective, we should reorder\n            //but this is only hit on a rare sad path and we\'d need to rejig the data structures to allow an efficient\n            //search\n            Timber.w("Removing current selection due to unexpected removal of cards");\n            onSelectNone();\n        }\n\n        updateList();\n    }\n\n    private CollectionTask.TaskListener mSuspendCardHandler = new searchCards();\n            endMultiSelectMode();\n            mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n            updatePreviewMenuItem();\n            invalidateOptionsMenu();    // maybe the availability of undo changed\n        }\n    };\n\n    private CollectionTask.TaskListener mSearchCardsHandler = new ListenerWithProgressBar() {\n        @Override\n        public void onPostExecute(TaskData result) {\n            if (result != null) {\n                mCards = result.getCards();\n                updateList();\n                handleSearchResult();\n            }\n            updatePreviewMenuItem();\n            hideProgressBar();\n        }\n\n\n        private void handleSearchResult() {\n            Timber.i("CardBrowser:: Completed doInBackgroundSearchCards Successfully");\n            updateList();\n            \n            if ((mSearchView == null) || mSearchView.isIconified()) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (hasSelectedAllDecks()) {\n                UIUtils.showSimpleSnackbar(CardBrowser.this, getSubtitleText(), true);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            //If we haven\'t selected all decks, allow the user the option to search all decks.\n            String displayText;\n            if (getCardCount() == 0) {\n                displayText = getString(R.string.card_browser_no_cards_in_deck, getSelectedDeckNameForUi());\n            } else {\n                displayText = getSubtitleText();\n            }\n            View root =           return getString(R.string.card_browser_all_decks);\n            }\n            return getCol().getDecks().name(lastDeckId);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            Timber.w(e, "Unable blocks the long-click callback while the task is running, so usage of the task should be minimized\n            mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n        }\n\n\n        @Override\n        public    List<Long> cardsIdsToHide = (List<Long>) result.getObjArray()[1];\n                        if (cardsIdsToHide.size() > 0) {\n                            Timber.i("Removing %d invalid cards from view", cardsIdsToHide.size());\n                            removeNotesView(cardsIdsToHide, true);\n                        }\n                    } catch (Exception e) {\n Timber.d("Completed doInBackgroundRenderBrowserQA Successfuly");\n            } else {\n                // Might want to do something more proactive here like show a message box?\n                Timber.e("doInBackgroundRenderBrowserQA was not successful... continuing anyway");\n            }\n        }\n\n\n        @Override\n        public void onCancelled() {\n            hideProgressBar();\n        }\n    };\n\n    private CollectionTask.TaskListener mCheckSelectedCardsHandler = new ListenerWithProgressBar() {\n        @Override\n        public void finishWithAnimation(ActivityTransitionAnimation.RIGHT);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Render the second column whenever the user stops scrolling\n     */\n    private final class RenderOnScroll implements AbsListView.OnScrollListener {\n        @Override\n        public void onScroll(AbsListView view, int firstVisibleItem, int visibleItemCount, int totalItemCount) {\n            // Show the progress bar if scrolling to given position requires rendering of the question / answer\n            int lastVisibleItem = firstVisibleItem + visibleItemCount;\n            int size = getCardCount();\n            if ((size > 0) && (firstVisibleItem < size) && ((lastVisibleItem - 1) < size)) {\n                String firstAns = getCards().get(firstVisibleItem).get(ANSWER);\n                // Note: max value of lastVisibleItem is totalItemCount, so need to subtract 1\n                String lastAns = getCards().get(lastVisibleItem - 1).get(ANSWER);\n                if (firstAns == null || lastAns == null) {\n                    showProgressBar();\n                    // Also start rendering the items on the screen every 300ms while scrolling\n                    long currentTime = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime ();\n                    if ((currentTime - mLastRenderStart > 300 || lastVisibleItem >= totalItemCount)) {\n                        mLastRenderStart = currentTime;\n                        CollectionTask.cancelTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_RENDER_BROWSER_QA);\n                        CollectionTask.launchCollectionTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_RENDER_BROWSER_QA, mRenderQAHandler,\n                                new CollectionTask.TaskData(new Object[]{getCards(), firstVisibleItem, visibleItemCount}));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void onScrollStateChanged(AbsListView listView, int scrollState) {\n            // TODO: Try change to RecyclerView as currently gets stuck a lot when using scrollbar on right of ListView\n            // Start rendering the question & answer every time the user stops scrolling\n            if (scrollState == SCROLL_STATE_IDLE) {\n                int startIdx = listView.getFirstVisiblePosition();\n                int numVisible = listView.getLastVisiblePosition() - startIdx;\n                CollectionTask.launchCollectionTask(CollectionTask.TASK_TYPE_RENDER_BROWSER_QA, mRenderQAHandler,\n                        new CollectionTask.TaskData(new Object[]{getCards(), startIdx - 5, 2 * numVisible + 5}));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private final class MultiColumnListAdapter extends BaseAdapter {\n        private final int mResource;\n        private String[] mFromKeys;\n        private final int[] mToIds;\n        private float mOriginalTextSize = -1.0f;\n        private final int mFontSizeScalePcent;\n        private Typeface mCustomTypeface = null;\n        private LayoutInflater mInflater;\n\n        public MultiColumnListAdapter(Context context, int resource, String[] from, int[] to,\n                                      int fontSizeScalePcent, String customFont) {\n            mResource = resource;\n            mFromKeys = from;\n            mToIds = to;\n            mFontSizeScalePcent = fontSizeScalePcent;\n            if (!"".equals(customFont)) {\n                mCustomTypeface = AnkiFont.getTypeface(context, customFont);\n            }\n            mInflater = (LayoutInflater) context.getSystemService(Context.LAYOUT_INFLATER_SERVICE);\n        }\n\n\n        public View getView(int position, View convertView, ViewGroup parent) {\n            // Get the main container view if it doesn\'t already exist, and call bindView\n            View v;\n            if (convertView == null) {\n                v = mInflater.inflate(mResource, parent, false);\n                final int count = mToIds.length;\n                final View[] columns = new View[count];\n                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n                    columns[i] = v.findViewById(mToIds[i]);\n                }\n                v.setTag(columns);\n            } else {\n                v = convertView;\n            }\n            bindView(position, v);\n            return v;\n        }\n\n\n        private void bindView(final int position, final View v) {\n            // Draw the content in the columns\n            View[] columns = (View[]) v.getTag();\n            final Map<String, String> card = getCards().get(position);\n            for (int i = 0; i < mToIds.length; i++) {\n                TextView col = (TextView) columns[i];\n                // set font for column\n                setFont(col);\n                // set text for column\n                col.setText(card.get(mFromKeys[i]));\n            }\n            // set card\'s background color\n            final int backgroundColor = Themes.getColorFromAttr(CardBrowser.this, getColor(card));\n            v.setBackgroundColor(backgroundColor);\n            // setup checkbox to change color in multi-select mode\n            final CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox) v.findViewById(R.id.card_checkbox);\n            // if in multi-select mode, be sure to show the checkboxes\n            if(mInMultiSelectMode) {\n                checkBox.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n                if (mCheckedCardPositions.contains(position)) {\n                    checkBox.setChecked(true);\n                } else {\n                    checkBox.setChecked(false);\n                }\n                // this prevents checkboxes from showing an animation from selected -> unselected when\n                // checkbox was selected, then selection mode was ended and now restarted\n                checkBox.jumpDrawablesToCurrentState();\n            } else {\n                checkBox.setChecked(false);\n                checkBox.setVisibility(View.GONE);\n            }\n            // change bg color on check changed\n            checkBox.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {\n                @Override\n                public void onClick(View view) {\n                    onCheck(position, v);\n                }\n            });\n        }\n\n        private void setFont(TextView v) {\n            // Set the font and font size for a TextView v\n            float currentSize = v.getTextSize();\n            if (mOriginalTextSize < 0) {\n                mOriginalTextSize = v.getTextSize();\n            }\n            // do nothing when pref is 100% and apply scaling only once\n            if (mFontSizeScalePcent != 100 && Math.abs(mOriginalTextSize - currentSize) < 0.1) {\n                // getTextSize returns value in absolute PX so use that in the setter\n                v.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_PX, mOriginalTextSize * (mFontSizeScalePcent / 100.0f));\n            }\n\n            if (mCustomTypeface != null) {\n                v.setTypeface(mCustomTypeface);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Get the background color of items in the card list based on the Card\n         * @param cardProperties -- a card object to color\n         * @return index into TypedArray specifying the background color\n         */\n        private int getColor(Map<String, String> cardProperties) {\n            boolean suspended = "True".equals(cardProperties.get(SUSPENDED));\n            int flag = getFlagOrDefault(cardProperties, 0);\n            boolean marked = cardProperties.get(MARKED) != null ;\n            switch (flag) {\n                case 1:\n                   return R.attr.flagRed;\n                case 2:\n                   return R.attr.flagOrange;\n                case 3:\n                  return R.attr.flagGreen;\n                case 4:\n                   return R.attr.flagBlue;\n                default:\n                    if (marked) {\n                        return R.attr.markedColor;\n                    } else {\n                        if (suspended) {\n                            return R.attr.suspendedColor;\n                        } else {\n                            return android.R.attr.colorBackground;\n                        }\n                    }\n            }\n        }\n\n\n        public void setFromMapping(String[] from) {\n            mFromKeys = from;\n            notifyDataSetChanged();\n        }\n\n\n        public String[] getFromMapping() {\n            return mFromKeys;\n        }\n\n\n        @Override\n        public int getCount() {\n            return getCardCount();\n        }\n\n\n        @Override\n        public Object getItem(int position) {\n            return getCards().get(position);\n        }\n\n\n        @Override\n        public long getItemId(int position) {\n            return position;\n        }\n\n    }\n\n    @VisibleForTesting\n    int getFlagOrDefault(Map<String, String> card, int defaultValue) {\n        String flagValue = card.get(FLAGS);\n        if (flagValue == null) {\n            Timber.d("Unable to obtain flag for card: \'%s\'. Returning %d", card.get(ID), defaultValue);\n            return defaultValue;\n        }\n        try {\n            return Integer.parseInt(flagValue);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "couldn\'t parse flag value: %s", flagValue);\n            return defaultValue;\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    private void onCheck(int position, View cell) {\n        CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox) cell.findViewById(R.id.card_checkbox);\n\n        if (checkBox.isChecked()) {\n            mCheckedCardPositions.add(position);\n        } else {\n            mCheckedCardPositions.remove(position);\n        }\n\n       onSelectionChanged();\n    }\n\n    private void onSelectAll() {\n        for (int i = 0; i < mCards.size(); i++) {\n            mCheckedCardPositions.add(i);\n        }\n        onSelectionChanged();\n    }\n\n    private void onSelectNone() {\n        mCheckedCardPositions.clear();\n        onSelectionChanged();\n    }\n\n    private void onSelectionChanged() {\n        Timber.d("onSelectionChanged()");\n        try {\n            if (!mInMultiSelectMode && !mCheckedCardPositions.isEmpty()) {\n                //If we have selected cards, load multiselect\n                loadMultiSelectMode();\n            } else if (mInMultiSelectMode && mCheckedCardPositions.isEmpty()) {\n                //If we don\'t have cards, unload multiselect\n                endMultiSelectMode();\n            }\n\n            //If we\'re not in mutliselect, we can select cards if there are cards to select\n            if (!mInMultiSelectMode && this.mActionBarMenu != null) {\n                MenuItem selectAll = mActionBarMenu.findItem(R.id.action_select_all);\n                selectAll.setVisible(mCards != null && cardCount() != 0);\n            }\n\n            if (!mInMultiSelectMode) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n            updateMultiselectMenu();\n            mActionBarTitle.setText(Integer.toString(mCheckedCardPositions.size()));\n        } finally {\n            mCardsAdapter.notifyDataSetChanged();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private List<Map<String, String>> getCards() {\n        if (mCards == null) {\n            mCards = new ArrayList<>();\n        }\n        return mCards;\n    }\n\n    private long[] getAllCardIds() {\n        long[] l = new long[mCards.size()];\n        for (int i = 0; i < mCards.size(); i++) {\n            l[i] = Long.parseLong(mCards.get(i).get(ID));\n        }\n        return l;\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * Show/dismiss dialog when sd card is ejected/remounted (collection is saved by SdCardReceiver)\n     */\n    private void registerExternalStorageListener() {\n        if (mUnmountReceiver == null) {\n            mUnmountReceiver = new BroadcastReceiver() {\n                @Override\n                public void onReceive(Context context, Intent intent) {\n                    if (intent.getAction().equals(SdCardReceiver.MEDIA_EJECT)) {\n                        finishWithoutAnimation();\n                    }\n                }\n            };\n            IntentFilter iFilter = new IntentFilter();\n            iFilter.addAction(SdCardReceiver.MEDIA_EJECT);\n            registerReceiver(mUnmountReceiver, iFilter);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * The views expand / contract when switching between multi-select mode so we manually\n     * adjust so that the vertical position of the given view is maintained\n     */\n    private void recenterListView(@NonNull View view) {\n        final int position = mCardsListView.getPositionForView(view);\n        // Get the current vertical position of the top of the selected view\n        final int top = view.getTop();\n        final Handler handler = new Handler();\n        // Post to event queue with some delay to give time for the UI to update the layout\n        handler.postDelayed(new Runnable() {\n            @Override\n            public void run() {\n                // Scroll to the same vertical position before the layout was changed\n                mCardsListView.setSelectionFromTop(position, top);\n            }\n        }, 10);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Turn on Multi-Select Mode so that the user can select multiple cards at once.\n     */\n    private void loadMultiSelectMode() {\n        if (mInMultiSelectMode) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Timber.d("loadMultiSelectMode()");\n        // set in multi-select mode\n        mInMultiSelectMode = true;\n        // show title and            case TASK_TYPE_DELETE_MODEL:\n                return  doInBackGroundDeleteModel(params);\n\n            case TASK_TYPE_DELETE_FIELD:\n                return doInBackGroundDeleteField(params);\n\n            case TASK_TYPE_REPOSITION_FIELD:\n                return doInBackGroundRepositionField(params);\n\n            case TASK_TYPE_ADD_FIELD:\n                return doInBackGroundAddField(params);\n\n            case TASK_TYPE_CHANGE_SORT_FIELD:\n                return doInBackgroundChangeSortField(params);\n\n            case TASK_TYPE_SAVE_MODEL:\n                return doInBackgroundSaveModel(params);\n            case TASK_TYPE_FIND_EMPTY_CARDS:\n                return doInBackGroundFindEmptyCards(params);\n            case TASK_TYPE_CHECK_CARD_SELECTION:\n                return doInBackgroundCheckCardSelection(params);\n\n            default:\n                Timber.e("unknown task type: %d", mType);\n                return null;\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    /** Delegates to the {@link TaskListener} for this task. */\n    @Override\n    protected void onPreExecute() {\n        super.onPreExecute();\n        if (mListener != null) {\n            mListener.onPreExecute(this);\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    /** Delegates to the {@link TaskListener} for this task. */\n    @Override\n    protected void onProgressUpdate(TaskData... values) {\n        super.onProgressUpdate(values);\n        if (mListener != null) {\n            mListener.onProgressUpdate(this, values);\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    /** Delegates to the {@link TaskListener} for this task. */\n    @Override\n    protected void onPostExecute(TaskData result) {\n        super.onPostExecute(result);\n        if (mListener != null) {\n            mListener.onPostExecute(this, result);\n        }\n        Timber.d("enabling garbage collection of mPreviousTask...");\n        mPreviousTask = null;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void onCancelled(){\n        if (mListener != null) {\n            mListener.onCancelled();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundAddNote(TaskData[] params) {\n col.getDb();\n            db.getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            try {\n                publishProgress(new TaskData(col.addNote(note)));\n                db.getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                db.getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "doInBackgroundAddNote - RuntimeException on adding note");\n            AnkiDroidApp.sendExceptionReport(e, "doInBackgroundAddNote");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(true);\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundUpdateNote(TaskData[] params) {\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundUpdateNote");\n        // Save the note\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        AbstractSched sched = col.getSched();\n        Card editCard = params[0].getCard();\n        Note editNote = editCard.note();\n        boolean fromReviewer = params[0].getBoolean();\n\n        try {\n            col.getDb().getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            try {\n                // TODO: undo integration\n                editNote.flush();\n                // flush card too, in case, did has been changed\n                editCard.flush();\n                if (fromReviewer) {\n                    Card newCard;\n                    if (col.getDecks().active().contains(editCard.getDid())) {\n                        newCard = editCard;\n                        newCard.load();\n                        // reload qa-cache\n                        newCard.q(true);\n                    } else {\n                        newCard = sched.getCard();\n                    }\n col.getDb().getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "doInBackgroundUpdateNote - RuntimeException on updating note");\n            AnkiDroidApp.sendExceptionReport(e, "doInBackgroundUpdateNote");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(true);\n    }\n\n    // same as doInBackgroundUpdateNote but for multiple notes\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundUpdateNotes(TaskData[] newCard = null;\n        Timber.i(oldCard != null ? "Answering card" : "Obtaining card");\n        try {\n            DB db = col.getDb();\n            db.getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            try {\n                if (oldCard != null) {\n                    Timber.i("Answering card %d", oldCard.getId());\n                    sched.answerCard(oldCard, ease);\n                }\n                newCard = sched.getCard();\n                if (newCard != null) {\n                    // render cards before locking database\n                    newCard._getQA(true);\n                }\n                publishProgress(new TaskData(newCard));\n                db.getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                db.getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch     return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(true);\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundLoadDeckCounts(TaskData... params) {\n CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        AbstractSched sched = col.getSched();\n        Object[] data = params[0].getObjArray();\n        Card card = (Card) data[0];\n        Collection.DismissType type = (Collection.DismissType) data[1];\n        Note note = card.note();\n        try {\n            col.getDb().getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            try {\n                sched.deferReset();\n                switch (type) {\n                    case BURY_CARD:\n                        // collect undo information\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] { col.getDirty(), note.cards(), card.getId() });\n                        // then bury\n                        sched.buryCards(new long[] { card.getId() });\n                        break;\n                    case BURY_NOTE:\n                        // collect undo information\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] { col.getDirty(), note.cards(), card.getId() });\n                        // then bury\n                        sched.buryNote(note.getId());\n                        break;\n                    case SUSPEND_CARD:\n                        // collect undo information\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] { card });\n                        // suspend card\n                        if (card.getQueue() == Consts.QUEUE_TYPE_SUSPENDED) {\n                            sched.unsuspendCards(new long[] { card.getId() });\n                        } else {\n                            sched.suspendCards(new long[] { card.getId() });\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    case SUSPEND_NOTE: {\n                        // collect undo information\n                        ArrayList<Card> cards = note.cards();\n                        long[] cids = new long[cards.size()];\n                        for (int i = 0; i < cards.size(); i++) {\n                            cids[i] = cards.get(i).getId();\n                        }\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] { cards, card.getId() });\n                        // suspend note\n                        sched.suspendCards(cids);\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case DELETE_NOTE: {\n                        // collect undo information\n                        ArrayList<Card> allCs = note.cards();\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] { note, allCs, card.getId() });\n                        // delete note\n                        col.remNotes(new long[] { note.getId() });\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n                // With sHadCardQueue set, getCard() resets the scheduler prior to getting the next card\n                publishProgress(new TaskData(col.getSched().getCard(), 0));\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "doInBackgroundDismissNote - RuntimeException on dismissing note, dismiss type %s", type);\n            AnkiDroidApp.sendExceptionReport(e, "doInBackgroundDismissNote");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(true);\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundDismissNotes(TaskData... params) {\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        AbstractSched sched = col.getSched();\n        Object[] data = params[0].getObjArray();\n        long[] cardIds = (long[]) data[0];\n        // query cards\n        Card[] cards = new Card[cardIds.length];\n        for (int i = 0; i < cardIds.length; i++) {\n            cards[i] = col.getCard(cardIds[i]);\n        }\n\n        Collection.DismissType type = (Collection.DismissType) data[1];\n        try {\n            col.getDb().getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            try {\n                switch (type) {\n                    case SUSPEND_CARD_MULTI: {\n                        // collect undo information\n                        long[] cids = new long[cards.length];\n                        boolean[] originalSuspended = new boolean[cards.length];\n                        boolean hasUnsuspended = false;\n                        for (int i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {\n                            Card card = cards[i];\n                            cids[i] = card.getId();\n                            if (card.getQueue() != Consts.QUEUE_TYPE_SUSPENDED) {\n                                hasUnsuspended = true;\n                                originalSuspended[i] = false;\n                            } else {\n                                originalSuspended[i] = true;\n                            }\n                        }\n\n                        // if at least one card is unsuspended -> suspend all\n                        // otherwise unsuspend all\n                        if (hasUnsuspended) {\n                            sched.suspendCards(cids);\n                        } else {\n                            sched.unsuspendCards(cids);\n                        }\n\n                        // mark undo for all at once\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] {cards, originalSuspended});\n\n                        // reload cards because they\'ll be passed back to caller\n                        for (Card c : cards) {\n                            c.load();\n                        }\n\n                        sched.deferReset();\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case FLAG: {\n                        int flag = (Integer) data[2];\n                        col.setUserFlag(flag, cardIds);\n                        for (Card c : cards) {\n                            c.load();\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case MARK_NOTE_MULTI: {\n                        Set<Note> notes = CardUtils.getNotes(Arrays.asList(cards));\n                        // collect undo information\n                        List<Note> originalMarked = new ArrayList<>();\n                        List<Note> originalUnmarked = new ArrayList<>();\n\n                        for (Note n : notes) {\n                            if (n.hasTag("marked"))\n                                originalMarked.add(n);\n                            else\n                                originalUnmarked.add(n);\n                        }\n\n                        CardUtils.markAll(new ArrayList<>(notes), !originalUnmarked.isEmpty());\n\n                        // mark undo for all at once\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] {originalMarked, originalUnmarked});\n\n                        // reload cards because they\'ll be passed back to caller\n                        for (Card c : cards) {\n                            c.load();\n                        }\n\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case DELETE_NOTE_MULTI: {\n                        // list of all ids to pass to remNotes method.\n                        // Need Set (-> unique) so we don\'t pass duplicates to col.remNotes()\n                        Set<Note> notes = CardUtils.getNotes(Arrays.asList(cards));\n                        List<Card> allCards = CardUtils.getAllCards(notes);\n                        // delete note\n                        long[] uniqueNoteIds = new long[notes.size()];\n                        Note[] notesArr = notes.toArray(new Note[notes.size()]);\n                        int count = 0;\n                        for (Note note : notes) {\n                            uniqueNoteIds[count] = note.getId();\n                            count++;\n                        }\n\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] {notesArr, allCards});\n\n                        col.remNotes(uniqueNoteIds);\n                        sched.deferReset();\n                        // pass back all cards because they can\'t be retrieved anymore by the caller (since the note is deleted)\n                        publishProgress(new TaskData(allCards.toArray(new Card[allCards.size()])));\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case CHANGE_DECK_MULTI: {\n                        long newDid = (long) data[2];\n\n                        Timber.i("Changing %d cards to deck: \'%d\'", cards.length, newDid);\n                        JSONObject deckData = col.getDecks().get(newDid);\n\n                        if (Decks.isDynamic(deckData)) {\n                            //#5932 - can\'t change to a dynamic deck. Use "Rebuild"\n                            Timber.w("Attempted to move to dynamic deck. Cancelling task.");\n                            return new TaskData(false);\n                        }\n\n                        //Confirm that the deck exists (and is not the default)\n                        try {\n                            long actualId = deckData.getLong("id");\n                            if (actualId != newDid) {\n                                Timber.w("Attempted to move to deck %d, but got %d", newDid, actualId);\n                                return new TaskData(false);\n                            }\n                        } catch (Exception e) {\n                            Timber.e(e, "failed to check deck");\n                            return new TaskData(false);\n                        }\n\n                        long[] changedCardIds = new long[cards.length];\n                        for (int i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {\n                            changedCardIds[i] = cards[i].getId();\n                        }\n                        col.getSched().remFromDyn(changedCardIds);\n\n                        long[] originalDids = new long[cards.length];\n\n                        for (int i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {\n                            Card card = cards[i];\n                            card.load();\n                            // save original did for undo\n                            originalDids[i] = card.getDid();\n                            // then set the card ID to the new deck\n                            card.setDid(newDid);\n                            Note note = card.note();\n                            note.flush();\n                            // flush card too, in case, did has been changed\n                            card.flush();\n                        }\n\n                        // mark undo for all at once\n                        col.markUndo(type, new Object[] {cards, originalDids});\n\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                    case RESCHEDULE_CARDS:\n                    case REPOSITION_CARDS:\n                    case RESET_CARDS: {\n                        // collect undo information, sensitive to memory pressure, same for all 3 cases\n                        try {\n                            Timber.d("Saving undo information of type %s on %d cards", type, cards.length);\n                            col.markUndo(type, new Object[] {deepCopyCardArray(cards)});\n                        } catch (CancellationException ce) {\n                            Timber.i(ce, "Cancelled while handling type %s, skipping undo", type);\n                        }\n                        switch (type) {\n                            case RESCHEDULE_CARDS:\n                                sched.reschedCards(cardIds, (Integer) data[2], (Integer) data[2]);\n                                break;\n                            case REPOSITION_CARDS:\n                                sched.sortCards(cardIds, (Integer) data[2], 1, false, true);\n                                break;\n                            case RESET_CARDS:\n                                sched.forgetCards(cardIds);\n                                break;\n                        }\n                        // In all cases schedule a new card so Reviewer doesn\'t sit on the old one\n                        col.reset();\n                        publishProgress(new TaskData(sched.getCard(), 0));\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "doInBackgroundSuspendCard - RuntimeException on suspending card");\n            AnkiDroidApp.sendExceptionReport(e, "doInBackgroundSuspendCard");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        // pass cards back so more actions can be performed by the caller\n        // (querying the cards again is unnecessarily expensive)\n        return new TaskData(true, cards);\n    }\n\n    private Card[] deepCopyCardArray(Card[] originals) throws CancellationException {\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(AnkiDroidApp.getInstance());\n        Card[] copies = new Card[originals.length];\n        for (int i = 0; i < originals.length; i++) {\n            if (isCancelled()) {\n                Timber.i("Cancelled during deep copy, probably memory pressure?");\n                throw new CancellationException("Cancelled during deep copy");\n            }\n\n            // TODO: the performance-naive implementation loads from database instead of working in memory\n            // the high performance version would implement .clone() on Card and test it well\n            copies[i] = new Card(col, originals[i].getId());\n        }\n        return copies;\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundUndo(TaskData... params) {\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        AbstractSched sched = col.getSched();\n        try {\n            col.getDb().getDatabase().beginTransaction();\n            Card newCard = null;\n            try {\n                long cid = col.undo();\n                if (cid == 0) {\n                    // /* card schedule change undone, reset and get\n                    // new card */\n                    Timber.d("Single card non-review change undo succeeded");\n                    col.reset();\n                    newCard = sched.getCard();\n                } else if (cid > 0) {\n                    // a review was undone,\n                     /* card review undone, set up to review that card again */\n                    Timber.d("Single card review undo succeeded");\n                    newCard = col.getCard(cid);\n                    newCard.startTimer();\n                    col.reset();\n                    col.getSched().decrementCounts(newCard);\n                    sched.deferReset();\n                } else {\n                    // cid < 0\n                    /* multi-card action undone, no action to take here */\n                    Timber.d("Multi-select undo succeeded");\n                }\n                // TODO: handle leech undoing properly\n                publishProgress(new TaskData(newCard, 0));\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().setTransactionSuccessful();\n            } finally {\n                col.getDb().getDatabase().endTransaction();\n            }\n        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n            Timber.e(e, "doInBackgroundUndo - RuntimeException on undoing");\n            AnkiDroidApp.sendExceptionReport(e, "doInBackgroundUndo");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(true);\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundSearchCards(TaskData... params) {\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundSearchCards");\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        String query = (String) params[0].getObjArray()[0];\n        Boolean order = (Boolean) params[0].getObjArray()[1];\n        int numCardsToRender = (int) params[0].getObjArray()[2];\n        List<Long> searchResult_ = col.findCards(query, order);\n        int resultSize = searchResult_.size();\n        List<Map<String,String>> searchResult = new ArrayList<>(resultSize);\n        Timber.d("The search found %d cards", resultSize);\n        for (Long cid: searchResult_) {\n            Map<String, String> card = new HashMap<>();\n            card.put(CardBrowser.ID, cid.toString());\n            searchResult.add(card);\n        }\n        // Render the first few items\n        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(numCardsToRender, searchResult.size()); i++) {\n            Card c = col.getCard(Long.parseLong(searchResult.get(i).get("id")));\n            CardBrowser.updateSearchItemQA(mContext, searchResult.get(i), c, col);\n        }\n        // Finish off the task\n        if (isCancelled()) {\n            Timber.d("doInBackgroundSearchCards was cancelled so return null");\n            return null;\n        } else {\n            return new TaskData(searchResult);\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundRenderBrowserQA(TaskData... params) {\n        //TODO: Convert this to accept the following to make thread-safe:\n        //(Range<Position>, Function<Position, BrowserCard>)\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundRenderBrowserQA");\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        List<Map<String, String>> cards = (List<Map<String, String>>) params[0].getObjArray()[0];\n        Integer startPos = (Integer) params[0].getObjArray()[1];\n        Integer n = (Integer) params[0].getObjArray()[2];\n\n        List<Long> invalidCardIds = new ArrayList<>();\n        // for each specified card in the browser list\n        for (int i = startPos; i < startPos + n; i++) {\n            // Stop if cancelled\n            if (isCancelled()) {\n                Timber.d("doInBackgroundRenderBrowserQA was aborted");\n                return null;\n            }\n            if (i < 0 || i >= cards.size()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            Map<String, String> card;\n            try {\n                card = cards.get(i);\n            }\n            catch (IndexOutOfBoundsException e) {\n                //even though we test against card.size() above, there\'s still a race condition\n                //We might be able to optimise this to return here. Logically if we\'re past the end of the collection,\n                //we won\'t reach any more cards.\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (card.get("answer") != null) {\n                //We\'ve already rendered the answer, we don\'t need to do it again.\n                continue;\n            }\n            // Extract card item\n            Card c;\n            String maybeCardId = card.get("id");\n            if (maybeCardId == null) {\n                Timber.w("CardId was null, skipping");\n                continue;\n            }\n            Long cardId;\n            try {\n                cardId = Long.parseLong(maybeCardId);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                Timber.e("Unable to parse CardId: %s. Unable to remove card", maybeCardId);\n                continue;\n            }\n            try {\n                c = col.getCard(cardId);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                //#5891 - card can be inconsistent between the deck browser screen and the collection.\n                //Realistically, we can skip any exception as it\'s a rendering task which should not kill the\n                //process\n                Timber.e(e, "Could not process card \'%s\' - skipping and removing from sight", maybeCardId);\n                invalidCardIds.add(cardId);\n                continue;\n            }\n            // Update item\n            CardBrowser.updateSearchItemQA(mContext, card, c, col);\n            float progress = (float) i / n * 100;\n            publishProgress(new TaskData((int) progress));\n        }\n        return new TaskData(new Object[] { cards, invalidCardIds });\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundCheckDatabase(TaskData... params) {\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundCheckDatabase");\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        // Don\'t proceed if collection closed\n        if (col == null) {\n            Timber.e("doInBackgroundCheckDatabase :: supplied collection was null");\n            return new TaskData(false);\n        }\n\n        Collection.CheckDatabaseResult result = col.fixIntegrity(new ProgressCallback(this, AnkiDroidApp.getAppResources()));\n        if (result.getFailed()) {\n            //we can fail due to a locked database, which requires knowledge of the failure.\n            return new TaskData(false, new Object[] { result });\n        } else {\n            // Close the collection and we restart the app to reload\n            CollectionHelper.getInstance().closeCollection(true, "Check Database Completed");\n            return new TaskData(true, new Object[] { result });\n        }\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundRepairDeck(TaskData... params) {\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundRepairDeck");\n        Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n        if (col != null) {\n            Timber.i("RepairDeck: Closing collection");\n            col.close(false);\n        }\n        return new TaskData(BackupManager.repairCollection(col));\n    }\n\n\n    private TaskData doInBackgroundUpdateValuesFromDeck(TaskData... params) {\n        Timber.d("doInBackgroundUpdateValuesFromDeck");\n        try {\n            Collection col = CollectionHelper.getInstance().getCol(mContext);\n            AbstractSched sched = col.getSched();\n            Object[] obj = params[0].getObjArray();\n            boolean reset = (Boolean) obj[0];\n            if (reset) {\n                // reset actually required because of counts, which is used in getCollectionTaskListener\n                sched.reset();\n            }\n            int[] counts = sched.counts();\n            int totalNewCount = sched.totalNewForCurrentDeck();\n            int totalCount = sched.cardCount();\n            return new TaskData(new AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='Both files compile and the app builds. Here\'s a summary of the defect and the fix.\n\n## Diagnosis\n\nThe reported flow is: Card Browser → multi-select two cards → **Edit** → change the note type → crash, with only the first card changed.\n\nTwo separate defects combine to produce it:\n\n**1. `CardBrowser.hasSelectedSingleNoteId()` was checking the wrong thing.** It guards the visibility of the multi‑select *Edit note* action, and was comparing `mCards.get(position).get(NOTE)`. But `NOTE` is not the note id — `updateSearchItemQA()` populates it with `c.model().optString("name")`, i.e. the **note type name** (the browser\'s "Note" column). Worse, that key is only populated for rows that have already been rendered; for the rest the map contains nothing but `ID`, so the value is `null`.\n\nConsequently, for two different notes that share the same note type (exactly the reporter\'s "two cards in Basic 1"), the set collapses to a single entry and the guard wrongly reports "one note selected". The Edit action is offered, `openNoteEditorForCurrentlySelectedNote()` opens the editor on `getSelectedCardIds()[0]` only — hence "only the first card is affected by the change".\n\n**2. Stale selection positions after the list is reloaded.** Changing the note type sets `reloadRequired`, so `onActivityResult()` calls `searchCards()` (which resets `mCards` to an empty list) and then `invalidateOptionsMenu()`. Because multi-select mode is still active with `mCheckedCardPositions = {0, 1}`, the menu rebuild runs `onSelectionChanged() → updateMultiselectMenu()`, which indexes the now-empty list — `IndexOutOfBoundsException`. The same stale indices are also handed to `doInBackgroundCheckCardSelection()`, which dereferences them on a background thread.\n\n## Fix\n\n- `hasSelectedSingleNoteId()` now resolves the *real* note ids with a single `select distinct nid from cards where id in (…)` query, so Edit is only offered when the selection genuinely belongs to one note.\n- `getSelectedCardIds()` skips positions that are no longer in range instead of throwing.\n- `searchCards()` clears `mCheckedCardPositions` when it discards the card list, since those indices refer to the list being thrown away (`onSelectionChanged()` then cleanly drops out of multi-select mode).\n- `updateMultiselectMenu()` passes snapshots of the selection and card list to the background task rather than the live, mutable collections, and `doInBackgroundCheckCardSelection()` ignores out-of-range positions defensively.\n\nThe batch note-type reassignment the reporter asked for is a feature request rather than the defect, so it is out of scope here; the correctness fix is that the editor is no longer offered for a multi-note selection, and the reload no longer crashes.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 1176, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 97030, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 1176, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 3, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYMdHX117rnK6Yztf2k4', stop_reason=None, session_id='23f0cd2a-a4f8-4485-9131-32550b418ccb', uuid='e38b1477-6d96-414b-a70f-0631d625651c')
